# Graph Report - ProjectCAD  (2026-11-01)

## Corpus Check
- 3185 files · ~12,487,565 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 1892 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .dxf 1345, .ui 170, .lff 50)

## Summary
- 83306 nodes · 167663 edges · 2079 communities (1717 shown, 362 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 16185 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f4306bf1`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
